## Common questions

### Where can I find LLM &amp; AI Agent Engineer jobs?

This list has 300 open LLM &amp; AI Agent Engineer roles from 210 companies, refreshed every 2 days from landed's live job database. Each role links to a landed job page with the employer's own application.

### Which companies are hiring for LLM &amp; AI Agent Engineer roles right now?

Top-rated companies hiring now include Anthropic, Cresta, ClickUp, Coinbase, NVIDIA, Zoom, Salesforce and Profound. 21 roles are at YC-backed companies.

Yes. 93 of the 300 roles are remote, marked 🌐 in the list.

### Are there entry-level LLM &amp; AI Agent Engineer jobs?

<details>
<summary><b>How this list is built</b></summary>

This list is generated from landed's live job database and refreshed every two days. It keeps open roles posted in the last 90 days that link to an employer application. US-based and worldwide-remote roles come first, then the rest of the world. Within each group, roles from top-rated companies (by TrueUp lists, Y Combinator, valuation, funding stage and investors) come first among roles posted in the last 30 days, then newer roles. Each company appears at most 5 times, and staffing agencies are left out. Company badges come from TrueUp and Y Combinator data.

Spotted a closed or wrong role? [Open an issue](https://github.com/landedjobs/llm-engineer-jobs/issues/new).

</details>
